\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}X_{\theta,h}^{(k)}\right\|^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}\right]=\textrm{Var}_{2}\left[\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}X_{\theta,h}^{(k)}\right|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}\right]

and

\displaystyle\textrm{Var}_{2}\left[\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}X_{\theta,h}^{(k)}\right|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}X_{\theta,h}^{(k)}\right\|^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}\right]=\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\textrm{Var}\left[\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right|s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4G^{2}(H-h)^{2}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right),

where we have used
\textrm{Var}\left[\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right|s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)},h\right]\leq 4G^{2}(H-h)^{2}
. Furthermore, notice that
\left\|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}X_{\theta,h}^{(k)}\right\|\leq 2G(H-h)\sqrt{C_{1}d}
, the remaining steps will be exactly the same as those in the proof of (
11
), combined with a union bound over
j\in[m]
. In this way, we have proved (
12
). Taking a union bound again finishes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Proofs of Main Theorems

Define
\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}:=\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\top}\right)\nu_{1}^{\theta}
. We may prove the following decomposition of
\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}
:

Lemma B.1
.

We have
\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}=E_{1}+E_{2}+E_{3}
, where

\displaystyle E_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\left[\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle E_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\left[\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle E_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\left[\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w_{h}^{\theta}\right].

The proof of Lemma
B.1
is deferred to appendix
C
. Based on this observation, here we show the proofs of our main theorems.

B.1
Proof of Theorem
6.2

Proof.

We use Lemma
B.1
to decompose
\langle\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}},t\rangle=\langle E_{1},t\rangle+\langle E_{2},t\rangle+\langle E_{3},t\rangle
. To bound each term individually, we introduce the following lemmas, whose proofs are deferred to appendix
C
.

Lemma B.2
.

For any
t\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle E_{1},t\rangle|\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\log(2/\delta)}{K}}+\frac{2\log(2/\delta)\sqrt{C_{1}md}\|t\|B}{3K}.

where
B=\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)\max_{j\in[m]}\sqrt{\left(\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}}+2G\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h)^{2}\sqrt{\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu^{\theta}_{h}}
.

Lemma B.3
.

Let
E_{2}^{j}
be the
j
th entry of
E_{2}
, suppose
K\geq 36\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}C_{1}dH^{2}\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}
and

\lambda\leq C_{1}d\min_{h\in[H]}\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{h})\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}
, then with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|E_{2}^{j}|\leq 240\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(2+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\sqrt{C_{1}}dH^{3}\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\frac{\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m].

Lemma B.4
.

Let
E_{3}^{j}
be the
j
th entry of
E_{3}
, suppose
K\geq 36\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}C_{1}dH^{3}
and

\lambda\leq C_{1}dH\min_{h\in[H]}\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{h})\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}
, with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|E_{3}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle 6C_{1}dH^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\frac{\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m].

Let
B_{1}^{j}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)\sqrt{\left(\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}},\ B_{2}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h)^{2}G\sqrt{\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu^{\theta}_{h}}
, then we have the relation
B=\max_{j\in[m]}B_{1}^{j}+2B_{2}
. For any
j\in[m]
, note that

\displaystyle B_{1}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right\|\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}H\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}H\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot

\displaystyle\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}\neq h^{\prime}}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}H\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\leq H^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right),

where we use the result of Lemma
A.5
. Similarly,

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h)^{2}G\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right\|\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}H^{2}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}H^{2}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\leq H^{3}G\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|.

We conclude that when
K\geq 36C_{1}dH^{2}\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\max_{j\in[m]}B_{1}^{j}+2B_{2}\right)\frac{2\log\frac{2}{\delta}\sqrt{C_{1}md}\|t\|}{K}\leq H^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\max_{j\in[m]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+2HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\frac{2\log\frac{2}{\delta}\sqrt{C_{1}md}\|t\|}{K},

and therefore, with probability
1-3\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle E_{1},t\rangle|+|\langle E_{2},t\rangle|+|\langle E_{3},t\rangle|\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\log(2/\delta)}{K}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(5+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\left(\max_{j\in[m]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\frac{240C_{1}dH^{3}\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}}{K}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\log(2/\delta)}{K}}+\kappa_{1}(5+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\left(\max_{j\in[m]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\frac{240C_{1}dH^{3}\sqrt{m}\|t\|\log\frac{24dmH}{\delta}}{K}.

replacing
\delta
by
\frac{\delta}{3}
, we have finished the proof.
∎

B.2
Proof of Theorem
6.3

Proof.

According to the result of Theorem
6.2
, we know

\displaystyle|\langle t,\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\rangle|\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t}{K}\cdot\log\frac{8}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{\theta}\|t\|\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},

Pick
t=e_{j},j\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}G^{2}(H-h)^{4}\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)^{2}\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left((H-h)^{4}G^{2}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}+(H-h+1)^{2}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}\right).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C_{1}d\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}+2C_{1}d\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}.

Define

\displaystyle\zeta_{h}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{h}^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a\right)+Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)\right)\mathrm{d}a

\displaystyle\eta_{h}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a\right)+Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)\right)\mathrm{d}a

\displaystyle-\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)-Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a_{h+1}^{(1)}\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right).

Note that the sequence
\zeta_{1},\eta_{1},\zeta_{2},\eta_{2}\ldots,\zeta_{H},\eta_{H}
forms a martingale difference sequence, therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\zeta_{h}^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}(\zeta_{h}^{2}+\eta_{h}^{2})\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\zeta_{h}+\eta_{h}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\nabla_{\theta}Q_{1}^{\theta}\left(s_{1}^{(1)},a_{1}^{(1)}\right)-\sum_{h=1}^{H}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a^{(1)}_{h+1}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a_{h+1}^{(1)}\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4H^{4}G^{2}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)-r_{h}^{(1)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s^{(1)}_{h+1}\right.\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s^{(1)}_{h+1},a\right)\mathrm{d}a\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a\left|s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right.\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a\right)\mathrm{d}a-Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)},a_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Q_{1}^{\theta}\left(s_{1}^{(1)},a_{1}^{(1)}\right)-\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{2}\right]\leq H^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\leq

\displaystyle 8C_{1}dH^{4}G^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}+2C_{1}dH^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}.

Therefore, taking a union bound over
j
, we get

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}\right|\leq 4b_{\theta}\sqrt{\frac{\min\left\{C_{1}d,H\right\}\log\frac{8m}{\delta}}{K}}+\frac{2C_{\theta}\sqrt{m}\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m],

where

\displaystyle b_{\theta}=H^{2}G\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+H\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|.

When we in addition have
\phi(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s,a)\geq 0,\forall h\in[H],(s,a),(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
, we have for any
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\left(\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\phi(s,a)\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\phi(s,a)\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}}(a_{h^{\prime}}|s_{h^{\prime}})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\phi(s,a)\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h}\left|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}}(a_{h^{\prime}}|s_{h^{\prime}})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Gh\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\phi(s,a)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Gh\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\phi(s,a),

which implies

\displaystyle\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\leq G^{2}h^{2}\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}G^{2}(H-h)^{4}\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}G^{2}h^{2}(H-h+1)^{2}\left(\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2H^{2}G^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)^{2}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C_{1}d\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}+2C_{1}dG^{2}H^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}

Repeating the steps that we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]
, we get

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}\right|\leq 4H^{2}G\sqrt{\frac{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}\log\frac{8m}{\delta}}{K}}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+\frac{2C_{\theta}\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m].

∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem
6.4

Proof.

When the MDP is tabular and
\phi
is the one-hot vector, we have

\displaystyle\nu^{\theta}_{h,s,a}=\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a),\quad\Sigma_{h}=\textrm{diag}(\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a))

which implies

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}\right)^{2}\right]+2G^{2}H^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}\right]+2G^{2}H^{2}\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H],s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}}\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}+2G^{2}H^{2}\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\right)^{2}\right]\max_{h\in[H],s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}}\frac{\mu_{\theta,h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}.

Following the same argument above, we can derive

\displaystyle t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\leq

\displaystyle 4H^{4}G^{2}\max_{h\in[H],s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}}\frac{\mu^{\theta}_{h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)},

i.e.,

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}\right|\leq 4H^{2}G\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8m}{\delta}}{K}}\max_{h\in[H],s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}}\frac{\mu^{\theta}_{h}(s,a)}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}+\frac{2C_{\theta}\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m].

On the other hand, the result of Theorem
6.3
implies

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}\right|\leq 4H^{2}G\sqrt{\frac{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}\log\frac{8m}{\delta}}{K}}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+\frac{2C_{\theta}\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m].

Using the relation
\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right\|=\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\frac{\left(\mu^{\theta}_{h}(s,a)\right)^{2}}{\bar{\mu}_{h}(s,a)}}=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\frac{\mu^{\theta}_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})}{\bar{\mu}(s_{h},a_{h})}}
, and taking minimum over the above two inequalities, we have finished the proof.
∎

B.4
Proof of Theorem
6.5

Proof.

We use the same decomposition as in Theorem
6.2
. Define a martingale difference sequence
\{e_{k}^{\theta}\}_{k=1}^{K}
by

\displaystyle e_{k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})\nabla_{\theta}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta},

we have

\displaystyle\|e_{k}^{\theta}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\max_{j\in[m]}\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\|\sqrt{C_{1}d}(H-h+1)+\frac{2}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\|\sqrt{C_{1}d}(H-h)^{2}G\rightarrow 0,

where we use the result of Lemma
A.2
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e_{k}^{\theta}\left(e_{k}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\right]_{ij}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{1}}^{i}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta_{1}}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta_{1}}\right)\right]\left[\nabla_{\theta_{2}}^{j}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta_{2}}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta_{2}}\right)\right]^{\top}\right]\Bigg|_{\theta_{1}=\theta_{2}=\theta}=\frac{[\Lambda_{\theta}]_{ij}}{K}.

Therefore,by WLLN, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[e_{k}^{\theta}\left(e_{k}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\right]_{ij}\rightarrow_{p}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{k}^{\theta}\left(e_{k}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\right]_{ij}=\left[\Lambda_{\theta}\right]_{ij},

To finish the rest of the proof, we introduce the following lemmas,

Lemma B.5

(Martingale CLT, Corollary 2.8 in (McLeish et al., 1974))
.

Let
\left\{X_{mn},n=1,\ldots,k_{m}\right\}
be a martingale difference array (row-wise) on the probability triple
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},P)
.Suppose
X_{mn}
satisfy the following two conditions:

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq n\leq k_{m}}\left|X_{mn}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\rightarrow}}0,\textrm{ and }\sum_{n=1}^{k_{m}}X_{mn}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\rightarrow}}\sigma^{2}

for
k_{m}\rightarrow\infty
. Then
\sum_{n=1}^{k_{m}}X_{mn}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,\sigma^{2}\right)
.

Lemma B.6

(Cramér–Wold Theorem)
.

Let
X_{n}=(X_{n}^{1},X_{n}^{2},\ldots,X_{n}^{k})^{\top}
be a
k
-dimensional random vector series and
X=(X^{1},X^{2},\ldots,X^{k})^{\top}
be a random vector of same dimension. Then
X_{n}
converges in distribution to
X
if and only if for any constant vector
t=(t_{1},t_{2},\ldots,t_{k})^{\top}
,
t^{\top}X_{n}
converges to
t^{\top}X
in distribution.

Lemma
B.5
implies
\sum_{k=1}^{K}t^{\top}e_{k}\rightarrow_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t)
for any
t
, and Lemma
B.6
implies

\displaystyle\sqrt{K}E_{1}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}e_{k}\rightarrow_{p}\mathcal{N}(0,\Lambda_{\theta}).

Furthermore, notice that the results of Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
imply
\sqrt{K}E_{2}\rightarrow_{p}0,\sqrt{K}E_{3}\rightarrow_{p}0
. Combining the above results, we have finished the proof.
∎

B.5
Proof of Theorem
6.6

Proof.

Our proof is similar to that of
[
8
]
. We first derive the influence function of policy gradient estimator for sake of completeness. We denote each of the
K
sampled trajectories as

\bm{\tau}:=\left(s_{1},a_{1},r_{1},s_{2},a_{2},r_{2},\ldots,s_{H},a_{H},r_{H},s_{H+1}\right)

We denote
\bar{\pi}(a\mid s)
as the behavior policy. The distribution of trajectory is then given by

\mathcal{P}(d\bm{\tau})=\bar{\xi}\left(ds_{1},da_{1}\right)p_{1}\left(ds_{2}\mid s_{1},a_{1}\right)\bar{\pi}_{2}\left(da_{2}\mid s_{2}\right)\ldots\bar{\pi}_{H}\left(da_{H}\mid s_{H}\right)p_{H}\left(ds_{H+1}\mid s_{H},a_{H}\right)

Define
p_{\eta}=p+\eta\Delta p
as a new transition probability function and
\mathcal{P}_{\eta}:=\mathcal{P}+\eta\Delta\mathcal{P}
where
\Delta\mathcal{P}
satisfies

(\Delta\mathcal{P}_{h})\mathcal{F}\subseteq\mathcal{F},\forall h\in[H].

Define
g_{\eta,h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s,a\right):=\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\log p_{\eta,h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s,a\right)
and the score function as

g_{\eta}(\bm{\tau}):=\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\log\mathcal{P}_{\eta}(d\bm{\tau})=\sum_{h=1}^{H}g_{\eta,h}\left(s_{h+1}\mid s_{h},a_{h}\right).

Without loss of generality, we assume
p_{\eta}
is continuously derivative with respect to
\eta.
This guarantees that we can change the order of taking derivatives with respect to
\eta
and
\theta.
When the subscript
\eta
vanishes, it means
\eta=0
and the underlying transition probability is
p(s^{\prime}|s,a),
i.e.
p_{0}(s^{\prime}|s,a)=p(s^{\prime}|s,a).
Then we denote
g_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a):=\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\log p_{\eta,h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\right|_{\eta=0},
and
g(\bm{\tau})=\sum_{h=1}^{H}g_{h}(s_{h+1}|s_{h},a_{h}).
We define the policy value under new transition kernel is

v_{\theta,\eta}:=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right]

Then, our objective function is

\psi_{\eta}:=\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta,\eta}=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta,h}\left(a_{h}\mid s_{h}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}r_{h}\left(s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}\right)\right)\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right].

We are going to compute the influence function with respect to the above objective function. We denote this influence function as
\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau}).
By definition, it satisfies that

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\psi_{\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}=\mathbb{E}\left[g(\bm{\tau})\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right].

By exchanging the order of derivatives, we find that

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\psi_{\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}=\nabla_{\theta}\left[\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}\right].

Therefore, we calculate the derivatives.

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\int_{(\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A})^{h}}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\xi(s_{1})\prod_{j=1}^{h-1}p_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{h}\pi_{\theta,j}\left(a_{j}\mid s_{j}\right)d\bm{\tau}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\int_{(\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A})^{h}}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\right)\xi(s_{1})\prod_{j=1}^{h-1}p_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{h}\pi_{\theta,j}\left(a_{j}\mid s_{j}\right)d\bm{\tau}_{h}

\displaystyle=\int_{(\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A})^{H}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\right)\left[\xi(s_{1})\prod_{j=1}^{H}p_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{H}\pi_{\theta,j}\left(a_{j}\mid s_{j}\right)\right]d\bm{\tau}.

We denote
Q_{h,\eta}^{\theta}
and
\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h,\eta}^{\theta}
as the state-action function and its gradient with underlying transition probability being
p_{\eta}.
For sake of simplicity, we define the state value function as

V_{h}^{\theta}(s):=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}r_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right|s_{h}=s,\mathcal{P}\right].

We denote
V_{h,\eta}^{\theta}(s)
as the same function except for transition probability substituted by
p_{\eta}.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\right)\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{j=1}^{H}g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\sum_{h=j+1}^{H}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{j=1}^{H}g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h=j+1}^{H}r_{h}\left(s_{h},a_{h}\right)\right|s_{j+1}\right]\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{j=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.g_{\eta,j}\left(s_{j+1}\mid s_{j},a_{j}\right)V_{j+1,\eta}^{\theta}(s_{j+1})\right|s_{j},a_{j}\right]\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right].

Therefore,

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.g_{h}\left(s_{h+1}\mid s_{h},a_{h}\right)V_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1})\right|s_{h},a_{h}\right]\right|s_{1}\sim\xi,\mathcal{P}_{\eta}\right].

(16)

Notice that
\Sigma_{h}=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})^{\top}\right]
and denote
w_{h}(s,a):=\phi^{\top}(s,a)\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu_{h}^{\theta}=\phi^{\top}(s,a)\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\mid s_{1}\sim\xi\right].
We leverage the following fact to rewrite (
16
): for any
f(s,a)=w_{f}^{\top}\phi(s,a)\in\mathcal{F}
where
w_{f}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[w_{f}^{\top}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[w_{f}^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\phi^{\top}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\right]\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[w_{f}^{\top}\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\phi^{\top}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[f(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\right]

Since

\mathbb{E}\left[g_{h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s,a\right)V_{h+1}^{\theta}(s^{\prime})|s,a\right]=\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\left(Q_{h,\eta}^{\theta}(s,a)-r_{\eta}(s,a)\right)\right|_{\eta=0}\in\mathcal{F},

we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\mathbb{E}\left[g_{h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\cdot V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s^{\prime}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim p(\cdot\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})g_{h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\cdot V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s^{\prime}\right)\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim p(\cdot\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})g_{h}\left(s^{\prime}\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s^{\prime}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s^{\prime}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})g_{h}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[g\left(\bm{\tau}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\left(V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right].

Taking gradient in both sides and we have

\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}v_{\theta,\eta}\right|_{\eta=0}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left\{g\left(\bm{\tau}\right)\cdot\nabla_{\theta}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\left(V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right]\right\}.

The implies that the influence function we want is

\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})=\nabla_{\theta}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{h}(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})\left(V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(1)}\right)\mid s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right].

Insert the expression of
w_{h}(s,a)
and exploit
\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}=Q_{h}^{\theta}(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})-r_{h}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\mid s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime},
we can rewrite the influence function as

\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})=-\nabla_{\theta}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\varepsilon_{h,1}^{\theta}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right]

Therefore, since the cross terms vanish by taking conditional expectation, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})^{\top}\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right]=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right)^{\top}\nabla_{\theta}\left(\varepsilon^{\theta}_{h,1}\phi(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)})^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)\Bigg]=\Lambda_{\theta}.

For any vector
t\in\mathbb{R}^{m},
when it comes to
\left\langle t,\psi_{\eta}\right\rangle,
by linearity we have

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\left\langle t,\psi_{\eta}\right\rangle\right|_{\eta=0}=\mathbb{E}[g(\bm{\tau})\left\langle t,\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right\rangle].

Then the influence function of
\left\langle t,\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\right\rangle
is
\left\langle t,\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right\rangle.
The Cramer-Rao lower bound for
\left\langle t,\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\right\rangle
is

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle t,\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right\rangle^{2}\right]=t^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})^{\top}\mathcal{I}(\bm{\tau})\right]t=t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t.

By continuous mapping theorem, a trivial corollary of Theorem
6.6
is that for any
t\in\mathbb{R}^{m},

\sqrt{K}\left(\left\langle t,\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\right\rangle\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\right).

This implies that the variance of any unbiased estimator for
\left\langle t,\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\right\rangle\in\mathbb{R}
is lower bounded by
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t.

∎

Appendix C
Missing Proofs

C.1
Proof of Proposition
4.3

Proof.

The differentiability of
w_{h}^{\theta}
comes from the differentiability of
Q_{h}^{\theta}
. And simply taking derivatives w.r.t.
\theta
on both sides of
Q_{h}^{\theta}=\phi^{\top}w_{h}^{\theta}
, we get the desired result.
∎

C.2
Proof of Proposition
5.1

Proof.

To prove the equality, it suffices to prove that given the same input
\widehat{Q}^{\theta}_{h+1},\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{Q}^{\theta}_{h+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathop{\arg\min}_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(f(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})-r_{h}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)^{2}+\lambda\rho(f)\right)=\widehat{r}_{h}+\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}

\displaystyle\mathop{\arg\min}_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(f(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\left(\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta,h+1}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\right)\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})+\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)^{2}+\lambda\rho(f)\right)

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\left(\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}+\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}_{h+1}}\right).

The second equation holds due to the definition of
\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}
. For the first equation, note that when
\mathcal{F}
is the class of linear functions and
\rho(\phi^{\top}w)=\|w\|^{2}
, the LHS has a closed form solution:

\displaystyle\mathop{\arg\min}_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(f(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)})-r_{h}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)^{2}+\lambda\rho(f)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(r_{h}^{(k)}+\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}r_{h}^{(k)}+\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta}(a^{\prime}|s_{h+1}^{(k)})\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime})\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{h}+\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}.

Therefore, we have finished the proof.
∎

C.3
Proof of Proposition
6.8

Proof.

The result of Theorem
6.3
implies for any fixed
\theta
, when we choose
\lambda\leq\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}C_{1}d\min_{h\in[H]}\sigma_{\textrm{min}}(\Sigma_{h})
, and
K\geq 36\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}C_{1}dH^{2}
sufficiently large such that

\displaystyle 4H^{2}G\sqrt{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{24m}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 480C_{1}dm^{0.5}H^{3.5}\kappa_{1}(5+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})(\max_{j\in[m]}\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\|+HG\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\|)\frac{\log\frac{72mdH}{\delta}}{K},

then we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\|\leq 8

\displaystyle H^{2}G\sqrt{m\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{24m}{\delta}}{K}}.

Note that when the diameter of
\Theta
is bounded by
D
, for any
\varepsilon>0
, it’s always possible to find an
\varepsilon
-net
\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
such that
|\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}|\leq\left(\frac{mD}{\varepsilon}\right)^{m}
. Taking a union bound over
\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
, we get with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\|\leq 16H^{2}Gm\sqrt{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{24mD}{\delta\varepsilon}}{K}},\quad\forall\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}.

Therefore, for any
\theta\in\Theta
, pick
\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
such that
\|\theta-\theta^{\prime}\|\leq\varepsilon
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2L\varepsilon+\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta^{\prime}}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta^{\prime}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2L\varepsilon+16H^{2}Gm\sqrt{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta^{\prime}},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{24mD}{\delta\varepsilon}}{K}}.

Because
\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta^{\prime}},\bar{\mu})
is
L^{\prime}
-Lipschitz in
\theta
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\|\leq 2L\varepsilon+16H^{2}Gm\sqrt{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+2L^{\prime}\varepsilon+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{24mD}{\delta\varepsilon}}{K}}.

In particular, pick

\displaystyle\varepsilon=\min\left\{\frac{1}{L^{\prime}},\frac{16H^{2}Gm}{L}\sqrt{\frac{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}{K}}\right\},

we get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}-\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}\|\leq 64H^{2}Gm\sqrt{\min\{C_{1}d,H\}}\sqrt{1+\chi^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\mu^{\theta},\bar{\mu})}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{24DKLL^{\prime}}{\delta HG}}{K}},\quad\forall\theta\in\Theta.

∎

C.4
Proof of Lemma
B.1

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}Q_{1}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\mathcal{P}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)U_{h}^{\theta}-\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\mathcal{P}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)U_{h}^{\theta}-\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}\right).

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h}^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left(\left(\nabla_{\theta}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)+\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}\nabla_{\theta}w_{h}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi^{\top}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi^{\top}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}\nabla_{\theta}w_{h}^{\theta}.

Using the definition of
\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}
, we get

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta,1}(a|s)\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{U_{h}^{\theta}}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\nabla_{\theta}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)\right)(s,a)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a\\
=&\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}w^{\theta}_{h}.\end{aligned}

(17)

For the second term, by Lemma
A.3
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h+1}\right)\left(Q_{h+1}^{\theta}-\widehat{Q}_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h+1}\right)\sum_{h^{\prime}=h+1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{h^{\prime}-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(Q_{h^{\prime}}^{\theta}-\widehat{r}_{h^{\prime}}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}Q_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{r}_{h}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right).

Meanwhile, again by Lemma
A.3
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)(Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{Q}_{1}^{\theta})=\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{r}_{h}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right),

which implies

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)(Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{Q}_{1}^{\theta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)+\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{r}_{h}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}-\widehat{r}_{h}-\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)-r_{h}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}w_{h}^{\theta}.

For each
j\in[m]
, notice the relation

\displaystyle\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{1}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)+\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(\nu_{1}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=1}^{h^{\prime}-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta,1}(a|s)\left(\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=1}^{h^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}\right)\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}=h^{\prime}+1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\phi^{\top}\right)(s,a)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left[\int\xi(s)\pi_{\theta,1}(a|s)\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\left(\widehat{U}_{h}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}_{h}^{\theta}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)(Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{Q_{1}^{\theta}})\right)(s,a)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a\right]_{j}\\
=&\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\left(Q_{h}^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)-r_{h}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)Q_{h+1}^{\theta}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)\\
&+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w_{h}^{\theta}\\
=&\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w_{h}^{\theta}.\end{aligned}

(18)

Combing the results of (
17
) and (
18
), we get for each
j\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}=\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta,1}(a|s)\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q_{1}^{\theta}}+\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\Pi_{\theta,1}\right)(Q_{1}^{\theta}-\widehat{Q_{1}^{\theta}})\right)(s,a)\textrm{d}s\textrm{d}a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\Bigg(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}_{h}+\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{K}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}w_{h}^{\theta}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w^{\theta}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{K}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w^{\theta}_{h}+\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right).

Rewriting the above decomposition in a vector form, we get

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\Bigg(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{K}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w^{\theta}_{h}+\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\nu_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\Bigg),

which is the desired result.
∎

C.5
Proof of Lemma
B.2

Proof.

Note that,

\displaystyle\langle E_{1},t\rangle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\langle\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\langle\left(\nabla_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta},t\right\rangle+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\langle\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta},t\right\rangle.

Let
e_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\langle\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle
, we have

\displaystyle|e_{k}|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h+1)\max_{j\in[m]}\sqrt{\left(\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}_{h}}+2G\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|\sum_{h=1}^{H}(H-h)^{2}\sqrt{\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\nu^{\theta}_{h}}=B\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|.

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\textrm{Var}[e_{k}]=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle^{2}\right]=Kt^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t.

We pick
\sigma^{2}=Kt^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t
, the Bernstein’s inequality implies that for any
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}e_{k}\right|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\varepsilon^{2}/2}{\sigma^{2}+\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B\varepsilon/3}\right).

Therefore, if we pick
\varepsilon=\sigma\sqrt{2\log(2/\delta)}+2\log(2/\delta)\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B/3
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}e_{k}\right|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq\delta,

i.e., we have with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}e_{k}\right|\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\log(2/\delta)}{K}}+\frac{2\log(2/\delta)\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B}{3K}

∎

C.6
Proof of Lemma
B.3

Proof.

For an arbitrarily given
\theta_{0}
, let
\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h}=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta_{0}}}[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\phi(s_{h},a_{h})^{\top}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0},1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{h}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}.

Taking derivatives on both sides, and let
\theta_{0}=\theta
, we get

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}E_{2}=\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right)=E_{21}^{j}+E_{22}^{j}+E_{23}^{j},

where

\displaystyle E_{21}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle E_{22}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle E_{23}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right|_{\theta_{0}=\theta}\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\theta_{0},h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right|_{\theta_{0}=\theta}\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}.

Therefore, using the result of Lemma
A.4
, we get

\displaystyle|E_{21}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle|E_{22}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle|E_{23}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}\neq h^{\prime}}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|,

where
\Delta\Sigma^{-1}_{h}=\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}_{h}-\Sigma_{h}
and we use the fact
\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta,h}\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 1
and
\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq G
from Lemma
A.5
. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|=\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\int_{\mathcal{A}}\phi\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\pi_{\theta,h+1}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}-M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}\left(\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta Y_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right),

where
\Delta Y_{\theta,h}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\phi_{\theta,h+1}\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right)^{\top}-\Sigma_{h}M_{\theta,h}
and the last inequality uses Lemma
A.4
again. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}\left(\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta Y_{\theta,h}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right).

Now, define
\alpha=6\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}
and pick

\displaystyle K\geq 36\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}C_{1}dH^{2}\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta},\quad\lambda\leq C_{1}d\min_{h\in[H]}\sigma_{\textrm{min}}(\Sigma_{h})\cdot\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta},

we get
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{H}
. Using the results of Lemma
A.6
, Lemma
A.7
, Lemma
A.8
, we get with probability
1-2\delta
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq&2\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}-I_{d}\right\|\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{2C_{1}d\log\frac{2dH}{\delta}}{K}}+\frac{4C_{1}d\log\frac{2dH}{\delta}}{3K}+\frac{2\lambda\|\Sigma^{-1}_{h}\|}{K}\\
\leq&4\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}\leq\alpha\leq 1,\end{aligned}

(19)

and
\forall j\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta Y_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 2(\kappa_{2}+1)\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}\leq 1,\quad\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta Y_{\theta,h}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 2(\kappa_{3}+1)\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}\leq 1,

which implies

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 2\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}\left(\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta Y_{\theta,h}\right)\Sigma_{h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\leq\alpha,

(20)

where we use the fact
(1+x_{1})(1+x_{2})-1\leq 2(x_{1}+x_{2})
whenever
x_{1},x_{2}\in[0,1]
. Similarly, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq\alpha,\quad\forall j\in[m].

(21)

Meanwhile, by Lemma
A.9
, we get with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{d}(H-h+1)\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}

(22)

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{h}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{d}G(H-h)^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}.

(23)

Combining the results of (
19
), (
20
), (
21
), (
22
),(
23
) and use a union bound, we have with probability
1-3\delta
,

\displaystyle|E_{21}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}16h\alpha\sqrt{d}G(H-h)^{2}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\alpha\sqrt{d}H^{4}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}},

\displaystyle|E_{22}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}8h\alpha\sqrt{d}(H-h+1)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\alpha\sqrt{d}H^{3}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle|E_{23}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}16Gh\alpha\sqrt{d}(H-h+1)(h-1)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\alpha\sqrt{d}H^{4}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}},

where we use the fact
(1+\alpha)^{h}-1\leq 2h\alpha
whenever
\alpha h\leq 1
. Summing up the above terms and using the definition of
\alpha
, we get

\displaystyle|E_{2}^{j}|\leq 240\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\sqrt{C_{1}}dH^{3}\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+HG\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K},\quad\forall j\in[m]

Replacing
\delta
by
\frac{\delta}{3}
, we have finished the proof.
∎

C.7
Proof of Lemma
B.4

Proof.

Similar to the decomposition in the proof of Lemma
B.3
, we have

\displaystyle\left|E_{3}^{j}\right|=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}_{h}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}_{h}^{-1}w_{h}^{\theta}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\prod_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}+1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\prod_{h^{\prime\prime}\neq h^{\prime}}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime\prime}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta,h^{\prime}}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta,h^{\prime}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right)\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\alpha\right)^{h}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+G(h-1)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|\right),

where
\alpha
is defined in the same way as that in the proof of Lemma
B.3
. Similarly, we have
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{H}
with probability
1-3\delta
and we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}_{h}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq G^{2}(H-h)^{4}

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(Q^{\theta}_{h}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq(H-h+1)^{2}.

We conclude

\displaystyle|E_{3}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle 3\frac{\lambda}{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|G(H-h)^{2}+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|(H-h+1)+G(h-1)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|(H-h+1)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\frac{\lambda}{K}H^{2}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{h}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|GH+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\frac{\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}C_{1}dH^{2}}{K}\max_{h\in[H]}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,h}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma_{h}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|GH+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta,1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right).

Replacing
\delta
by
\frac{\delta}{3}
, we have finished the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Extension to Time-homogeneous Discounted MDP

D.1
Approach

Our method can be easily extended to the case of time-homogeneous discounted MDP. Similar to the time-inhomogeneous case, under the setting of the time-homogeneous discounted MDP, an instance of MDP is defined by
(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{A},p,r,\xi,\gamma)
where
\mathcal{S}
and
\mathcal{A}
are the state and action spaces,
\gamma\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
is the discount factor,
p:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is the transition probability,
r:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow[0,1]
is the reward function and
\xi:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is the initial state distribution. Similarly, the policy
\pi:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is a distribution over the action space conditioned on an arbitrary given state
s
. We define the value function and
Q
function by

\displaystyle v^{\theta}=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}r(s_{h},a_{h})\right],\quad Q^{\theta}(s,a)=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}r(s_{h},a_{h})\right|s_{1}=s,a_{1}=a\right]

Note that here the reward and Q function no longer contain the subscript
h
. We still consider the class of linear functions
\mathcal{F}
with state-action feature
\phi
, and denote
\mathcal{P}_{\theta}
as the transition operator where
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
is the parameter of the policy.

Assumption D.1
.

For any
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we have
\mathcal{P}_{\theta}f\in\mathcal{F}
, and we suppose
r\in\mathcal{F}
.

In addition, we assume that the constant function belongs to
\mathcal{F}
, i.e., there exists some
w_{0}
such that
\phi(s,a)^{\top}w_{0}=1,\forall s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}
. Define the covariance matrix
\Sigma
and its empirical version
\widehat{\Sigma}
by

\displaystyle\Sigma:=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h}\right)\phi\left(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h}\right)^{\top}\right],\quad\widehat{\Sigma}:=\frac{1}{HK}\left(\lambda I_{d}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)^{\top}\right),

where
I_{d}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
is the identity matrix.

Assumption D.2

(Boundedness Conditions)
.

Assume
\Sigma
is invertible. There exist absolute constants
C_{1},G
such that for any
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A},j\in[m]
, we have

\displaystyle\phi(s,a)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\phi(s,a)\leq C_{1}d,\quad\left|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\right|\leq G.

Define
\widehat{w}_{r}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\widehat{M_{\theta}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d},\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}M_{\theta}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d},j\in[m]
by

\displaystyle\widehat{w}_{r}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)r_{h}^{(k)},

\displaystyle\widehat{M_{\theta}}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)\phi\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)^{\top}\mathrm{d}a^{\prime},

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta}}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\int_{\mathcal{A}}\phi\left(s_{h+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)^{\top}\nabla_{\theta}\pi_{\theta}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime},\quad j\in[m].

In this way, one can compute

\displaystyle\widehat{Q}^{\theta}(\cdot,\cdot)=\phi(\cdot,\cdot)^{\top}\widehat{w}^{\theta},\quad\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}}(\cdot,\cdot)=\phi(\cdot,\cdot)^{\top}\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{w}^{\theta}=\left(I_{d}-\gamma\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{-1}\widehat{w}_{r},\quad\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}}=\left(I_{d}-\gamma\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta}}\widehat{w}^{\theta}.

Then the estimator is derived from

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}=\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\left(\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}Q^{\theta}}(s,a)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\right)\widehat{Q}^{\theta}(s,a)\right)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a.

D.2
Results

Define
\nu^{\theta}_{h}:=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\phi(s_{h},a_{h})|s_{1}\sim\xi\right],\nu^{\theta}=\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\nu^{\theta}_{h}
and
\Sigma_{\theta}:=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta}}\left[\left.\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}\right|s\sim\xi_{\theta},a\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot|s)\right]
where
\xi_{\theta}
is the stationary distribution under
\pi_{\theta}
. Define
