\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\gamma\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)\left(\gamma\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h-h^{\prime}-1}\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)},a_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}\right)\left(Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)},a_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}\right)-r_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}-\gamma\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h^{\prime}+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h^{\prime}+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\gamma\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)\left(\gamma\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h-h^{\prime}-1}\phi^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}.

For each
j\in[m]
, notice the relation

\displaystyle\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu_{1}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\left(\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{h-1}+\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\left(\nu_{1}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\left(\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta}}\right)\left(\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{h-h^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\left(\sum_{h^{\prime}=0}^{h-1}\left(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h-h^{\prime}-1}\phi^{\top}\right)(s,a)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left[\int\xi(s)\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\left(\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\left(\gamma\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{\theta}\right)^{h-1}\left(\widehat{U}^{\theta}-\tilde{U}^{\theta}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)(Q^{\theta}-\widehat{Q}^{\theta})\right)(s,a)\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}a\right]_{j}\\
=&\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)},a_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}\right)\left(Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)},a_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}\right)-r_{h^{\prime}}^{(k)}-\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\theta}\left(a^{\prime}\left|s_{h^{\prime}+1}^{(k)}\right.\right)Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h^{\prime}+1}^{(k)},a^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}a^{\prime}\right)\\
&+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}_{h}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}\\
=&\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}.\end{aligned}

(31)

Combing the results of (
30
) and (
31
), we get for each
j\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}v_{\theta}}=\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\xi(s)\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}}+\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\log\Pi_{\theta}\right)(Q^{\theta}-\widehat{Q}^{\theta})\right)(s,a)\textrm{d}s\textrm{d}a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}+\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}+\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right).

Rewriting the above decomposition in a vector form, we get

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}-\widehat{\nabla_{\theta}v_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}\Bigg(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}+\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\Bigg),

which is the desired result.
∎

D.7.2
Proof of Lemma
D.19

Proof.

Note that,

\displaystyle\langle E_{1},t\rangle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\left(\nabla_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta},t\right\rangle+\left\langle\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta},t\right\rangle.

Let
e_{h,k}=\left\langle\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle
, we have

\displaystyle|e_{h,k}|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\max_{j\in[m]}\sqrt{\left(\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\nabla^{j}_{\theta}\nu^{\theta}}+2G\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\nu^{\theta}}=B\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|.

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\textrm{Var}[e_{h,k}|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}]=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\langle\nabla_{\theta}\left(\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right),t\right\rangle^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{h,k}\right]=HKt^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t.

We pick
\sigma^{2}=HKt^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t
, the Bernstein’s inequality implies that for any
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}e_{h,k}\right|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\varepsilon^{2}/2}{\sigma^{2}+\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B\varepsilon/3}\right).

Therefore, if we pick
\varepsilon=\sigma\sqrt{2\log(2/\delta)}+2\log(2/\delta)\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B/3
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}e_{h,k}\right|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq\delta,

i.e., we have with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{4}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}e_{h,k}\right|\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t^{\top}\Lambda_{\theta}t\log(8/\delta)}{HK}}+\frac{2\log(8/\delta)\sqrt{C_{1}dm}\|t\|B}{3HK}

∎

D.7.3
Proof of Lemma
D.20

Proof.

For an arbitrarily given
\theta_{0}
, let
\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}=\mathbb{E}^{\pi_{\theta_{0}}}[\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}|s\sim\xi_{\theta_{0}},a\sim\pi_{\theta_{0}}(\cdot|s)]
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\widehat{M}_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\left(M_{\theta}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}.

Taking derivatives on both sides, and let
\theta_{0}=\theta
, we get

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}^{j}E_{2}=\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\left(\nu^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\right)\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right)=E_{21}^{j}+E_{22}^{j}+E_{23}^{j},

where

\displaystyle E_{21}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle E_{22}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}

\displaystyle E_{23}^{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{M}_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\right|_{\theta_{0}=\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left.\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta_{0}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\right|_{\theta_{0}=\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}.

Therefore, using the result of Lemma
D.9
, we get

\displaystyle|E_{21}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(1-\gamma-\gamma\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-\left(1-\gamma\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(1-\frac{\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}{1-\gamma}\right)^{-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle|E_{22}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h^{\prime}-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left(1-\frac{\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}{1-\gamma}\right)^{-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle|E_{23}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=2}^{\infty}(h^{\prime}-1)\gamma^{h^{\prime}-1}G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h^{\prime}-2}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{G}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left(1-\frac{\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}{1-\gamma}\right)^{-2}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{HK}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|,

where
\Delta\Sigma^{-1}=\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}-\Sigma
and we use the fact
\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}M_{\theta}\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 1
and
\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq G
from Lemma
D.10
. Now, define
\alpha=6\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{16dmH}{\delta}}{K}}
and pick

\displaystyle K\geq 36\kappa_{1}(4+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})^{2}\frac{C_{1}d}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{16dmH}{\delta},\quad\lambda\leq C_{1}d\sigma_{\textrm{min}}(\Sigma)\cdot\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta},

we get
\alpha\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
. Using the results of Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
D.15
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}d\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{K}}\leq\alpha\leq 1,

(32)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha,

(33)

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha,\quad\forall j\in[m].

(34)

Meanwhile, the event
\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
implies

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{d}}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH}}

(35)

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{KH}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\phi\left(s_{h}^{(k)},a_{h}^{(k)}\right)\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\varepsilon_{h,k}^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\sqrt{d}G}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH}}.

(36)

Combining the results of (
32
), (
33
), (
34
), (
35
), (
36
) and use a union bound, we have with probability
1-3\delta
,

\displaystyle|E_{21}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16\alpha\sqrt{d}G}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH}}

\displaystyle|E_{22}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\alpha\sqrt{d}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH}}

\displaystyle|E_{23}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16G\alpha\sqrt{d}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH}}

where we use the fact
(1-\frac{\alpha}{1-\gamma})^{-1}(1+\alpha)\leq 1+\frac{3\alpha}{1-\gamma}
whenever
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}
. Summing up the above terms and using the definition of
\alpha
, we get

\displaystyle|E_{2}^{j}|\leq\frac{240\sqrt{\kappa_{1}}(2+\kappa_{2}+\kappa_{3})\sqrt{C_{1}}d}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|+\frac{G}{1-\gamma}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\frac{\log\frac{32dmH}{\delta}}{KH},\quad\forall j\in[m],

which finished the proof.
∎

D.7.4
Proof of Lemma
D.21

Proof.

Similar to the decomposition in the proof of Lemma
D.20
, we have

\displaystyle\left|E_{3}^{j}\right|=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{KH}\left|\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\left(\widehat{\nu}^{\theta}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1}w^{\theta}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h-1}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}(h-1)G\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)^{h-2}\left(1+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\left(\Delta M_{\theta}\right)}{G}\right)\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left(1+\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta\Sigma^{-1}\right)\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\right)\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{KH}\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\gamma^{h-1}\left(1+\alpha\right)^{h}\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w_{h}^{\theta}\right\|+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|+G(h-1)\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{KH(1-\gamma)}\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}\right\|+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|+\frac{2G}{1-\gamma}\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|\right),

where
\alpha
is defined in the same way as that in the proof of Lemma
D.20
. Similarly, we have
\alpha\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
and we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}w^{\theta}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\nabla_{\theta}^{j}Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq\frac{G^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}w^{\theta}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(Q^{\theta}\left(s_{h}^{(1)},a_{h}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

We conclude

\displaystyle|E_{3}^{j}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda}{KH(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\frac{3G}{1-\gamma}+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6C_{1}d\sigma_{\textrm{min}}(\Sigma)\cdot\log\frac{8dmH}{\delta}}{KH(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left\|\Sigma^{-1}\right\|\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Sigma^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left(\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\frac{G}{1-\gamma}+\left\|\Sigma_{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{\theta}^{j}\nu^{\theta}_{1}\right\|\right).

which has finished the proof.
∎
